- Produced: tests/Ims.Api.Tests (620 tests, real HTTP pipeline through WebApplicationFactory against a fresh SQL Server database per run; one collection fixture, unique data per test). Layout: Harness/ (fixture, test DB, host factory), Clients/ (typed ApiClient groups), Builders/ (ProductBuilder, DocumentBuilder, StockScenario, AccountFactory, SettingsOverride, ApprovalRuleScope), Support/ (Unique, Xlsx, EndpointCatalog + RoleMatrix, Concurrently, ForgedTokens), Tests/ (Auth, Rbac, MasterData, Documents, Concurrency, Audit, Import, Reports, Paging, Contract, Composition). - Result on current src/: 608 pass, 12 fail (all 12 are the defects below; the 3 concurrency ones are intermittent, 1-3 fail per run). Failing tests are kept red on purpose.
- DEFECT T1 (MAJOR) lost capacity under contention: `src/Ims.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs:54` gives up after 5 optimistic retries, so parallel work on the same hot row returns 409 "The data changed..." instead of waiting. Cases: 40 parallel issue postings on two products -> 1-3 get 409 (`ParallelPostingTests.Forty_parallel_issues_...`); 20 mixed receipts+issues on one product -> 1-9 get 409 (`Receipts_and_issues_posted_together_...`); 20 parallel document creates in one warehouse -> 1-2 get 409 (`DocumentNumberingTests.Twenty_parallel_creates_...`). No lost update, no 500, no deadlock, stock == ledger every time, so this is availability, not corruption. Fix: take the lock instead of retrying (`UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK` read of the StockBalance rows in ProductId order and of the DocumentSequence row, or sp_getapplock per warehouse), keep retry for real races.
- DEFECT T2 (MINOR) wrong audit "before": `PostDocumentHandler.cs:25` hard-codes `Status = Submitted` even when the document was Approved (`AuditTrailTests.Every_document_status_change_...`).
- DEFECT T3 (MINOR) reject audit has no status change: `RejectDocumentHandler.cs:12` records only `{ Reason }`; AUTH-03 wants before/after (Submitted -> Draft) (`AuditTrailTests.Rejecting_and_cancelling_...`).

- DEFECT T5 (MAJOR for the frontend contract) `X-Correlation-Id` header is missing on every response produced by `ApiExceptionHandler` (400/401/404/409 from handlers): ExceptionHandlerMiddleware clears headers set by `CorrelationIdMiddleware.cs:13`. ### 2026-10-11 be-framework -> fe-framework, orchestrator, ui-ux-designer, qa-power-supervisor (money precision: PR `feature/avg-cost-and-money-precision-2`)
(Inserted here, not at the end of the log, so that the three pull requests of the re-test round merge without a conflict in this file; the entry of the round, with the average-cost and the other backend contract changes, is the last one in the log.)
- Produced: the product-owner decisions on money. **Money has exactly 2 decimals; a cost per base unit derived from a pack price is a unit price with up to 4.** Code: `Ims.Domain.Money`, `StockDocumentLine.Amount` and `EnteredUnitCost` (stored, migration `StoredLineAmount`), `DocumentLineBuilder`, `ResponseShaper`, `ReportColumn`, `ClosedXmlExcelWriter/Reader`. Rules: `docs/architecture/data-and-performance-rules.md` section 11 (which field is money, which a unit price, which a quantity).
- Decisions others must respect: (1) a line amount is STORED, computed once (receipt: entered quantity x entered unit cost; average-valued line: base quantity x average) and a document total is the sum of the stored amounts, so a carton line is never re-priced through the per-base cost (100,000 cartons of 7 at 10.00 are 1,000,000.00). (2) The per-base cost stays for stock valuation. (3) Everything a person sees is rounded half away from zero; the internal average is not money (`docs/architecture/backend.md`). (4) A unit price is never multiplied by a quantity to make an amount; use `amount` and `value`.
- Needs from fe-framework: adapt `web/` to the section below, regenerate the client. Needs from product: nothing (the unit-price decision is implemented as option A).

#### Contract changes for the frontend (money precision)
- **`DocumentLineDto` (every document read and write).** `unitCost` is now the cost per ENTERED unit (the unit in `uomCode`), money, 2 decimals: what was typed, so `qty x unitCost` is the line `amount`. New `baseUnitCost` (the cost per BASE unit, a unit price, up to 4 decimals: 1 carton of 3 at 10.00 is 3.3333; a carton of 1,000 at 3.00 is 0.0030) and `amount` (the stored line amount, money). All three are `null` without `cost.view`. **The editor must load `unitCost` into the cost field and send it back as `unitCost`; never `baseUnitCost`.** (The editor used to load the per-base value, which re-priced a line in a non-base unit on every save: 1 carton of 3 at 10.00 came back as 3.33.) **A line valued at the average (every issue line; a receipt saved by someone who may not enter cost) has `costFromAverage: true` and `unitCost: null`**: there is no cost to edit. Show its `baseUnitCost` (the average) and `amount` read-only and send no cost for it: a `PUT` where the cost is omitted keeps the line at the average, so a Warehouse staff receipt is never re-priced by someone who opens and saves it (an average of 0.0030 stays 0.0030, 1.2349 x 1,000 stays 1,234.90). A cost typed on such a line makes it a typed cost (`costFromAverage` false). Known and unchanged from main: a Warehouse staff re-save of a draft replaces a cost a Supervisor typed on it with the average (staff may not enter cost). The amount of a flagged draft is re-valued at the current average on every save and at post, so a draft read earlier can be stale.
- **Totals.** `totalAmount` (document and list) is the sum of the line `amount`s, each rounded to a cent. The web footer total (a floating-point sum of `qty x unitCost` over the lines) can differ from the API by floating-point noise or, for a sub-cent product, by a cent, and it is wrong for a line at the average (`unitCost` is null there): the footer is the sum of the API line `amount`s (and `totalAmount` after a save); for a line being edited and not saved yet, `round(qty x unitCost, 2)` in decimal arithmetic, half away from zero. A line amount above 999,999,999,999,999 is a `400` ("The amount of a line cannot be more than 999,999,999,999,999.").
- **Money fields, 2 decimals:** `amount`, `totalAmount`, stock `value`, `totalValue`, approval `amountThreshold` and the trigger numbers of an amount rule, `unitCost` of a document line. **Unit prices, up to 4 decimals:** `baseUnitCost`, stock row `avgCost`, ledger `unitCost`, movements `unitCost`. Show a unit price with up to 4 decimals (trailing zeros may be trimmed to 2), an amount with exactly 2.
- **Input.** A receipt line `unitCost` with more than 2 decimals is a `400` "Unit cost can have at most 2 decimal places." with one field error per line (`Data.Lines[n].UnitCost`) for a caller who may enter cost (Clerk, Supervisor, Owner, Admin). It is NOT judged on an issue or on a request of the Warehouse staff, where the cost is ignored. A negative cost is a `400` with one message ("Unit cost cannot be negative."). A line with a TYPED cost whose amount would exceed 999,999,999,999,999 is a `400` ("The amount of a line cannot be more than 999,999,999,999,999."); a line valued at the average is capped at that figure instead, never refused (a 400 that depends on the hidden average would tell the Warehouse staff its size). A quantity whose base quantity exceeds 99,999,999,999,999 is a `400` ("Quantity is too large or has too many decimal places."); before, 1,000,000,000 cartons with a factor of 1,000,000 overflowed `decimal(18,4)` with a 500. Approval `amountThreshold` with more than 2 decimals: `400` "An amount can have at most 2 decimal places.". Limit the inputs to 2 decimals (`step="0.01"`).
- **Imports.** The opening-stock and product imports read the number in a cell, not the text it shows (a cell formatted `0.0` that holds 2.505 is refused; 1,234.50 is accepted; a formula is read by its result). A sub-cent cost is a row error.
- **Excel.** Money cells ("Value", "Amount", the template's "UnitCost") are numbers formatted `#,##0.00`; unit-price cells ("Avg cost" and "Unit cost" of the stock summary, ledger and movements exports) are numbers formatted `#,##0.0000`. The cells hold the rounded numbers.
- **Interaction with the average-cost change** (`feature/avg-cost-and-money-precision`): that change keeps the average and the cost per base unit with 10 decimals inside the database; this one decides what leaves the API. Deployed alone, this change rounds the 6-decimal values of today; deployed with it, the values are rounded from 10 decimals; nothing a screen shows has more places in either case.
- **Data.** Migration `StoredLineAmount` adds `StockDocumentLine.Amount`, `EnteredUnitCost` and `CostFromAverage` and fills them. Open (Draft, Submitted, Approved) receipt lines with a typed cost get `Amount = round(entered quantity x entered cost, 2)` and `EnteredUnitCost` = the cost spread back over the entered quantity; issue lines are flagged `CostFromAverage` with `Amount = round(base quantity x average, 2)`; an open receipt line whose cost per entered unit is not an exact 2-decimal value (more than 0.00005 away from it, or non-zero but below half a cent, such as 0.0004) is flagged too and keeps `round(base quantity x cost, 2)`, so its price is never lost or turned into a typed zero. Posted, reversed and cancelled lines keep `round(base quantity x cost, 2)` (no posted total moves). `docs/dba/16-check-subcent-costs.sql` lists the open receipt lines flagged because of a fraction of a cent.

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, integration-test-engineer, be-dba, business-analyst, orchestrator
- Produced: the API contract gaps 1-8 of the fe-framework entry (branch `feature/api-contract-gaps`). Code: `src/Ims.Api/Composition/{WireJson,OpenApiSetup,OperationIdTransformer,FileResponseTransformer,MultipartRequiredTransformer,FormFileComponentTransformer,EnumSchemaTransformer}.cs`, `src/Ims.Api/Common/{UtcDateTimeConverter,UtcNullableDateTimeConverter,ProducesFileAttribute}.cs`, document/stock/import/lookup changes in `src/Ims.Application/Features/*` and `src/Ims.Domain/Stock/Models/StockDocument.cs`, migration `20261010045608_DocumentFlagsAndReturnInfo` (Up and Down tested). Tests: Domain 474, Architecture 51, Api 728 (new: `Tests/Contract/{OpenApiDocumentTests,UtcTimestampTests,WarehouseNameOnStockTests}`, `Tests/Documents/{DocumentFlagsTests,DocumentCountsTests,DocumentConcurrencyTokenTests}`, extended import and lookup tests). `tests/smoke/smoke.mjs` has a new "API contract" block. Docs: `docs/architecture/backend.md` (API contract section), `docs/architecture/data-and-performance-rules.md` (section 9 index, new section 10 Time).
- Needs from fe-framework: adapt `web/` to the section below, then delete the rewrites in `web/scripts/fetch-openapi.mjs` that are now no-ops (see "OpenAPI"), regenerate the client (`npm run api:generate`), switch Home/inbox/Mine to the new fields and endpoint.
- Needs from be-dba: `IX_StockDocument_PostedAt` is new (filtered `REC_DELETED = 0 AND PostedAt IS NOT NULL`, includes Status and WarehouseId; consumer: "posted today" in `GET /api/documents/counts`) and is narrower than the candidate in `docs/dba/09-rewrite-candidates.sql`; reconcile it with R3 when you ship the movements rewrite. `IX_StockDocument_RejectedBy` is the FK index of the new `RejectedBy` column. Please run `docs/dba/07-maintenance.sql` after this migration on a big database (four columns added to `StockDocument`).
- Needs from integration-test-engineer: review the new tests; `Support/EndpointCatalog.cs` has `GET documents counts`.
- Needs from business-analyst: clerk partner rights (see "Not done").

#### Contract changes for the frontend
**OpenAPI document** (`/openapi/v1.json`, still OpenAPI 3.1; the 3.1 to 3.0 flavour rewrites and `additionalProperties: false` in `fetch-openapi.mjs` are still needed, everything below is now correct at the source)
- Enums (`DocumentStatus`, `DocumentType`, `IssuePurpose`, `LedgerReason`, `PartnerType`, `ProductSort`, `StockSort`, `AttachmentOwnerType`, `ImportRowStatus`, ...) are `{ "type": "string", "enum": [names] }`. `null` is never in the list: a nullable enum property is `oneOf: [{type: null}, {$ref}]`. The wire format is unchanged (names).
- `operationId` is `Tag_action` on every operation (unique, tag = controller, action = camelCase C# action name; a test pins the ones the client uses). Compared with the ids `fetch-openapi.mjs` derived, only these change: `Import_products` -> `Import_importProducts`, `Import_openingStock` -> `Import_importOpeningStock`, `Reports_stockSummaryExport|movementsExport|ledgerExport|lowStockExport` -> `Reports_exportStockSummary|exportMovements|exportLedger|exportLowStock`, `Attachments_create` -> `Attachments_upload`, `Attachments_get` -> `Attachments_download`; new: `Documents_counts`. All other ids are identical.
- `IFormFile` is no longer a component: an upload is `multipart/form-data` with an inline `{type: string, format: binary}` part named `file`, listed in `required`.
- Downloads (`/api/reports/*/export`, `/api/import/templates/{kind}`, `/api/attachments/{id}`) declare a 200 response with a binary body (`application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`, or `application/octet-stream` for attachments) and the `Content-Disposition` response header.
- Numbers are `{type: integer|number, format}`; the `["integer","string"]` arrays and the digit `pattern` are gone.
- Every `DateTime` is `{type: string, format: date-time}` and always UTC with a trailing `Z` (see below).
- CORS now also exposes the `ETag` header.

**Timestamps**: every instant (`createdAt`, `submittedAt`, `approvedAt`, `postedAt`, `returnedAt`, ledger `occurredAt`, audit `at`, movements `postedAt`, login `expiresAt`, ...) is `yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss[.fffffff]Z`. Remove the "assume UTC" patching in the client. Date-only filters (`from`, `to`) are unchanged: Kuala Lumpur calendar days.

**Documents**
- `GET /api/documents` (`DocumentListItem`): new fields `createdById`, `createdByName` (display name), `submittedById`, `submittedBy` (user name), `submittedByName`, `submittedAt`, `requiresApproval`, `requiresOverride`, `isLarge`, `isReturned`, `concurrencyToken`. `createdBy` is still the user name. New filters `mine=true` (created by me) and `requiresApproval=true|false`; the approvals inbox is `status=Submitted&requiresApproval=true` (sorted newest first like every list). Use `includeTotal=false` for badges you do not need exact.
- `GET /api/documents/counts` (new, `stock.read`): `{ returnedToMe, myDrafts, postedToday, approvalsWaiting }` for the signed-in user inside their warehouses. `myDrafts` includes the returned ones; `postedToday` is the Kuala Lumpur day, whoever posted, reversed documents included; `approvalsWaiting` = Submitted documents held for approval that I could act on (own submissions excluded unless self-approval is on; Owner sees only `isLarge`, Supervisor only not-large, Admin all, other roles 0). At most four indexed COUNT queries.
- `DocumentDto`: new `requiresOverride`, `isLarge`, `isReturned`, `createdById`, `createdByName`, `submittedByName`, `approvedByName`, `postedByName`, `returnedById`, `returnedBy` (user name), `returnedByName`, `returnedAt`, `concurrencyToken`. Meaning: `requiresOverride` = an issue was submitted below zero stock because negative stock is allowed (set at submit; stays true after posting if an override was used); `isLarge` = the approval rule that fired needs a role above Supervisor (set at submit); `isReturned` = Draft sent back by a reviewer (`rejectionReason` says why, `returned*` who and when). Submitting again clears `rejectionReason` and `returned*`; rejecting clears `requiresApproval`, `isLarge` and `requiresOverride` (a Draft has none of them).
- Each `DocumentLineDto` has `available` (number, base units, on hand in the document's warehouse now; compare with `baseQty`, lines of one product add up). It is `null` once the document is Posted, Reversed or Cancelled.
- Concurrency (E4): every response that returns a document has the `ETag` header (`"<concurrencyToken>"`), and the token changes on any change to the header, status or lines. `PUT /api/documents/{id}` and all action endpoints (submit, approve, reject, cancel, post, reverse) accept `If-Match: "<token>"`; a stale or unknown token is `409` (title "Conflict", detail "{no} was changed by someone else since you opened it...") and nothing is changed; no header or `*` = as before. The list item's `concurrencyToken` equals the full document's, so an open editor can detect a stale copy from a list refresh.

**Stock**: `StockRow`, `LowStockAlert` and `LedgerRow` have `warehouseName` next to `warehouseCode`.

**Import** (`POST /api/import/products|opening-stock`): every `ImportRowResult` has `status` (`New` | `Update` | `Error`); a preview now fills `created` and `updated` with what a commit would do (opening stock: every valid row is `New`, `updated` = 0); a commit reports the same statuses and counts. Matching is by SKU, case-insensitive. `ok` is unchanged.

**Product lookup**: `GET /api/products/lookup?code=` is always 200 with `{ found, product }`; an unknown code is `{ found: false, product: null }` (no more 404). Breaking for the old shape: `product` was the body before.

**Behaviour you may notice**: the submit audit payload now also records `isLarge` and `requiresOverride`; Reject now records who and when on the document.

#### Intentionally unchanged
- Login: lockout is still indistinguishable from a wrong password (E7 "try again in N minutes" stays impossible by design).
- Clerk and partners (item 6): not granted at the time; SUPERSEDED on 2026-10-10 (product owner decided: Clerk may create partners, see the last entry of this log). `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 1.1 reads the PRD matrix as "Clerk: partners only" but marks it as an open question (Q1, still unanswered in this log); the PRD text is garbled there; `Permissions.cs`, the Domain tests and the independent `RoleMatrix` in the integration tests all say Clerk has only `masterdata.read`; and partners share `masterdata.manage` with products and warehouses, so the minimum extra right would be a new permission (`partners.manage`), a product decision. business-analyst: please answer Q1; then it is a small change (new permission, `PartnersController` policy, tests).

#### Not done (and why)
- Ledger running balance (gap 8, second half): not in the scope list; it needs a window function over the ledger or a stored balance per entry (be-dba decision).
- Error-list download endpoint for imports (UX section 8): the rows with errors are already in the response; no change.
- RESOLVED by the entry "override reason at approval" at the bottom of this log (the reason is now entered at Approve). Old text: the override reason is taken when POSTING (`PostRequest.negativeStockOverrideReason`), not when approving as `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` D5/3.2 says; I did not change that flow. Open conflict for ui-ux-designer and be-system-designer: either the design follows the code (supervisor enters the reason at Post, already allowed and tested), or Approve takes a reason and stores it.
- `/api/documents/counts` has no `lowStockCount`: use `GET /api/stock/low-stock?pageSize=1` (total).

- Decisions others must respect: the wire format lives in `WireJson.cs` (one place); new enums need no registration; a new download needs `[ProducesFile]`; documents must keep moving ROW_VERSION when their lines change (do not bypass `AppDbContext`); flags `isLarge` / `requiresOverride` are facts about the moment of submission, not live values (live shortage = `available` vs `baseQty`).
- Open conflicts: override reason at Post vs Approve (resolved, see the bottom entry); `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 1.2 (Clerk and partners) vs `Permissions.cs` (above).

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, security-engineer, integration-test-engineer, orchestrator
- Produced: SEC-34 API side (`src/Ims.Api/Features/Auth/*`: `RefreshTokenCookie`, `CsrfGuard`, `AuthOptions`, `AuthController`; `Composition/AuthCookieSetup.cs`, `AllowedOrigins.cs`), security response headers (`Common/SecurityHeaders*.cs`, `Composition/SecurityHeadersSetup.cs`), SEC-18 (`Ims.Domain/Attachments/Services/ImageMetadataStripper.cs`, `IUploadScanner` + `UploadScanRequest/Result` in Application, `NoOpUploadScanner` in Infrastructure, `UploadAttachmentHandler` reads once, strips, scans, then stores), `.github/workflows/dast.yml` + `.github/zap/rules.tsv`, docs `docs/security/{refresh-cookie,web-headers,upload-scanning,dast}.md`, status log in `docs/security/security-review-initial.md`, `TASKS.md` Q13/Q14. Tests: domain +22, API +54 (`RefreshCookieTests`, `RefreshTokenCookieEnvironmentTests`, `SecurityHeadersTests`, `UploadSecurityTests`).
- Needs from fe-framework: migrate the web app to the cookie following `docs/security/refresh-cookie.md` section 5 (credentials on API calls, `X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest` on refresh and logout, no refresh token in `localStorage`, session check = one refresh call on start-up, regenerate the client), then ask the orchestrator to set `Auth__ReturnRefreshTokenInBody=false`. Also: production build `optimization.styles.inlineCritical=false` so the CSP in `docs/security/web-headers.md` does not block the stylesheet `onload`.
- Needs from security-engineer: triage the first DAST run (accepted findings go to `.github/zap/rules.tsv` with a reason); decide the AV engine for `IUploadScanner`.
- Needs from integration-test-engineer: nothing blocking; sections 6 items 39 and 43 (headers) are covered now.
- Decisions others must respect: (1) a request whose refresh token comes from the cookie must carry `X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest` and an `Origin`/`Referer` listed in `Cors:Origins` (403 otherwise); a body token needs neither. (2) `Cors:Origins` must list the web origin even for a same-origin deployment, no wildcard (start-up fails). (3) The cookie is `__Secure-ims-refresh` (Path `/api/auth`, no `__Host-`); Secure everywhere except Development over plain http. (4) `AuthResult.RefreshToken` and `RefreshRequest.RefreshToken` are now nullable (null/absent = use the cookie); refresh with neither returns 401, an explicit empty string still 400. (5) CORS now allows credentials. (6) Attachments: images are stored without metadata, so the stored size/SHA-256 differ from the uploaded file; a broken image is 400.
- Open conflicts: none. The parallel API-contract change touches DTO/OpenAPI areas; the only shared files I edited are `Messages.cs` (3 constants), `ApiConfigKeys.cs`, `HeaderNames.cs`, `PresentationServices.cs` (CORS + 2 registration lines), `PipelineSetup.cs` (1 line), `ApiFixture.cs`, `AuthClient.cs`, `ApiResponse.cs` (1 method).

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, integration-test-engineer, ui-ux-designer, orchestrator
- Produced: override reason at approval (branch `feature/override-reason-at-approve`, product-owner decision; `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` D5 / 3.2 already described it). Code: `StockDocument.Approve(..., overrideReason)` (domain rule `ReasonRequiredRule`, `Messages.OverrideReasonRequired`), `ApproveRequest`, `ApproveDocumentCommand/Validator/Handler`, `PostDocumentCommand` without a body, `PostingContext` reads the reason from the document, `SubmitDocumentHandler`. Removed: `PostRequest`, `PostDocumentValidator`. Tests: `NegativeStockTests` (rewritten), `OverrideReasonPlacementTests` (architecture), state-machine / posting-engine / strategy tests, approval, audit, flags and OpenAPI tests, `tests/smoke/smoke.mjs` (new override section).
- Needs from fe-framework: move the reason field from the Post call to the Approve call (below), show the "Approve with stock override" dialog when `requiresOverride && status === 'Submitted'`, drop the reason field from the Post dialog, regenerate the client (`PostRequest` disappears from the schema; `ApproveRequest` is new).
- Needs from integration-test-engineer: review; no new endpoint, so `EndpointCatalog` is unchanged.
- Needs from ui-ux-designer: nothing; the code now matches D5 / 3.2 (reason min 5 characters is still a UI-only rule: the API requires a non-blank reason, max 500).
- Decisions others must respect: (1) the override reason belongs to Approve and is stored on the document (`negativeStockOverrideReason`, column unchanged, no migration); posting never takes a reason. (2) An issue submitted below zero stock now ALWAYS requires approval (`requiresApproval = true`), whatever the value thresholds say, because the approver is the one who gives the reason (UX 3.2 step 2: "regardless of value thresholds"). Before, such a document could be posted straight away by a supervisor. (3) `requiresOverride` is still a fact about the moment of submission (true from submit, it stays true after approval and posting); the override is satisfied once `negativeStockOverrideReason` is set (status `Approved`). (4) Self-approval, approval limit (role rank vs the rule that fired), warehouse scope, and "warehouse staff cannot post approval-required documents" are unchanged.
- Open conflicts: none. Known edge: a document approved before this change with `requiresOverride = true` has no stored reason, so posting it while stock is still short fails with the "override reason ... approver gives when approving" error; there is no production data, cancel or recreate it.

#### Contract changes for the frontend
**`POST /api/documents/{id}/approve`** (permission `document.approve`, unchanged)
- Old: no request body. New: optional JSON body `{ "negativeStockOverrideReason": string | null }` (schema `ApproveRequest`; an empty body is still accepted).
- Required when the document has `requiresOverride = true`: a missing, empty or whitespace-only value gives `400` (ProblemDetails `detail`: "A reason is required to approve a document that takes stock below zero.") and the document stays `Submitted`. Longer than 500 characters gives `400` with `errors.negativeStockOverrideReason`. The value is trimmed before it is stored.
- Ignored (not stored) when the document does not require an override.
- Response: `DocumentDto` as before, now with `status: "Approved"` and `negativeStockOverrideReason` filled for an override. The audit entry `document.approved` records the approver (actor) and `negativeStockOverrideReason` in its after-payload.
- Errors unchanged: self-approval `400`, above the approval limit `403`, outside the warehouse scope `403`, wrong status `400`, stale `If-Match` `409`.

**`POST /api/documents/{id}/post`**
- Old: body `{ "negativeStockOverrideReason": string | null }` (schema `PostRequest`). New: no request body; `PostRequest` is removed from the OpenAPI document. Chosen: removed cleanly, not deprecated, because only our web app calls it. A client that still sends the old body is not broken: the body is ignored (a reason sent at Post is never stored).
- An override is posted with the reason recorded at approval. If stock is short at posting time and no reason was recorded (stock fell short after submission, so `requiresOverride` was false), posting fails with `400` ("Posting below zero stock needs an override reason, which the approver gives when approving."), unchanged status code, new wording.
- Response and audit (`document.posted` with `OverrideUsed`) unchanged; the reason is no longer written by Post.

**`DocumentDto` / `DocumentListItem`**: no field added or removed. Meaning changes only: `requiresOverride` (true from submit; the document now always has `requiresApproval = true` as well), `negativeStockOverrideReason` (set at approval, no longer at posting; visible to every `stock.read` user as before). `allowedActions` is unchanged: `approve` for a Submitted override appears for Supervisor/Owner/Admin who are not the submitter; `post` appears only after approval.

**`POST /api/documents/{id}/submit`**: response now has `requiresApproval = true` for every issue that `requiresOverride`.

- Produced: new permission `partners.create` (`Permissions.PartnersCreate`) granted to Clerk, Supervisor and Admin; `POST /api/partners` now requires it; `PUT /api/partners/{id}` (edit and deactivate) still requires `masterdata.manage` (Supervisor and Admin only). Duplicate protection: `PartnerNameTakenSpec` + pre-check in `CreatePartnerHandler` and `UpdatePartnerHandler` (409 `ConflictException`, `Messages.PartnerExists`), and migration `UniquePartnerNamePerType` (`IX_Partner_Type_Name` is now UNIQUE with `WHERE [REC_DELETED] = 0`; `Down` restores the plain index; `Up` first renames existing live duplicates by appending ` (duplicate <id>)` to all but the oldest so a populated database can migrate). Docs: `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 1.1, 1.2, S34 and Q1. Tests: `PermissionsTests` (domain), `RoleMatrix`/`EndpointCatalog` (POST partners now needs `partners.create`), `PartnerTests`, `AuditTrailTests`, `LoginTests`.
- Needs from fe-framework: show the New partner button when `permissions` contains `partners.create`; show row edit and deactivate only when it contains `masterdata.manage`; handle 409 on create and edit by showing the problem `detail` (for example "A supplier named 'X' already exists.") on the name field.
- Needs from integration-test-engineer: review the new tests (including the concurrent duplicate-create test).
- Decisions others must respect: (1) `POST /api/partners` still answers 200 with the `PartnerDto` (like every create in this API), not 201. (2) The duplicate rule is name + type, trimmed, case-insensitive under the database collation; an inactive partner still holds its name (reactivate instead of re-creating); the same name may exist once as supplier and once as customer. (3) Every partner creation is audited by the existing row-level audit (`RowCreated`, entity `Partner`, actor = the signed-in user); nothing new was needed.

#### Contract changes for the frontend
- `permissions` on login, refresh and `GET /api/auth/me` can now contain `"partners.create"`: Clerk, Supervisor, Admin have it; Owner and Warehouse staff do not. `masterdata.manage` (Supervisor, Admin) still gates edit and deactivate of partners, products and warehouses. Clerk therefore sees New but no row edit or deactivate.
- `POST /api/partners`: 200 for Clerk, Supervisor, Admin; 403 for Owner and Warehouse staff; 409 (title "Conflict") when a live partner of the same type already has the same name (trimmed, case-insensitive). Body shape unchanged.
- `PUT /api/partners/{id}`: same as before (Supervisor, Admin; 403 for Clerk); now also 409 when the new name and type collide with another partner.
- Open conflicts: none. Supersedes the "Clerk and partners" notes in the 2026-10-09 entry.

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, integration-test-engineer, ui-ux-designer, orchestrator (QA backend fixes, part 1 of 4)
- Produced (branch `feature/qa-backend-fixes-1`, `src/`, `tests/`, docs): who may do what with a document. Findings fixed: EA-01, EA-02, PS-07, PS-09 / EA-10, PS-10 / EA-11, EA-25, EA-05, PS-16 (posted today). Parts 2 (amounts, reversal message), 3 (low stock, import, audit search) and 4 (validation limits, If-Match, lockout, users, rate limit, the rest) follow as stacked branches `feature/qa-backend-fixes-2` to `-4`; the per-finding status list `docs/qa/fix-status-backend.md` comes with part 4. Code: `DocumentActionPolicy` (ownership, review routing; one source for `allowedActions`, `canAct` and the command guards) + `DocumentActionContext`, `ApprovalPolicy.MayReview`, `IApprovalEvaluator.ReviewRefusalAsync`, `IDocumentAccess.EnsureMayEditOrSubmit/EnsureMayCancel/EnsureDocumentInScope`, `ReasonRuleExtensions` (min 5 characters), `StockDocument.Reject` takes `allowSelfApproval`, `PostedBetweenCountSpec`, `Auth:ReturnRefreshTokenInBody` default false. Tests: domain +47, API +37 (`ReviewRoutingTests`, `OwnDocumentsTests`, `ReasonLengthTests`, updated `AllowedActionsTests`, `WarehouseScopeTests`, refresh-cookie default), existing tests that approved with the Owner or used 1-4 character reasons now use a Supervisor and real reasons.
- Needs from fe-framework: use `canAct` on list items instead of the client mirror of the reviewer rule; a document of another warehouse now answers 404 (was 403); the reason errors arrive under `errors.reason` / `errors.negativeStockOverrideReason` with "Enter a reason of at least 5 characters."; regenerate the client.
- Needs from ui-ux-designer: answer the open question added to `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 7.2 (a document that is both Large and override: Owner or Admin only?).
- Decisions others must respect: (1) Review routing: Admin reviews all, Owner only `isLarge`, Supervisor only not `isLarge`; the same rule gates Approve, Reject, the "waiting" count, `canAct` and `allowedActions`. (2) Warehouse staff and Clerk edit/submit/cancel only their own drafts and recall only what they submitted; Admin and Supervisor act on any. (3) `PostedToday` excludes reversed documents. (4) `Auth:ReturnRefreshTokenInBody` is false by default in code and `appsettings.json`: remove any `Auth__ReturnRefreshTokenInBody=true` from QA/compose environments (the test host sets it true on purpose for the legacy-body session tests).
- Open conflicts: none with existing code; the web app's reviewer mirror needs the change above.

#### Contract changes for the frontend (part 1)
- `GET /api/documents` (`DocumentListItem`): new `canAct` (bool): the caller may approve or reject this Submitted document now (held for approval, routed to the caller's role, not their own submission unless self-approval is on). `allowedActions` of a single document accounts for the same rule plus the live approval rules, so it never offers an action the API refuses.
- `GET /api/documents/counts`: `postedToday` no longer counts documents reversed since posting (equals the Posted list for today).
- `POST /api/documents/{id}/approve|reject`: `403` for an Owner on a document that is not Large ("Owners review only large documents. A supervisor reviews this one."); `403` for a Supervisor on a Large one ("above your approval limit"). Reject is a review: `400` "You cannot reject a document you submitted." for the submitter while self-approval is off (was 200).
- `POST /api/documents/{id}/reject`, `/reverse`, `/approve` (override reason): a reason is at least 5 characters after trimming, else `400` with `errors.reason` / `errors.negativeStockOverrideReason` = "Enter a reason of at least 5 characters." (blank: the existing "required" sentence). Reasons are stored trimmed. An override reason sent for a document that needs none must also be 5+ characters if present.
- `PUT /api/documents/{id}`, `/submit`, `/cancel`: `403` for Warehouse staff or Clerk on a draft they did not create ("You can only change documents you created.") or on a Submitted document they did not submit ("You can only cancel documents you submitted."). Documents in other statuses keep their state errors (400).
- Any document operation (read, edit, submit, approve, reject, cancel, post, reverse) on a document of a warehouse outside the caller's scope: `404` "Document not found." (was `403`). Creating a document in a foreign warehouse is still `403`.
- Login and refresh: `refreshToken` in the body is `null` by default (cookie only).

- Produced (branch `feature/qa-backend-fixes-2`, based on part 1): PS-03 (unposted issue amounts, why approval) and PS-01 (reversal blocked message). Code: `StockDocument.EstimateIssueCosts` + `IIssueValuation` (Create, Update and Submit value an issue at the current average cost), `ApprovalRule.Triggers` + `ApprovalTrigger` / `ApprovalMetric`, `IApprovalEvaluator.TriggersAsync`, `DocumentStatuses.IsOpen`, `IPostingEngine.FindReversalDeficits` + `IPostingStrategy.StockChangeOnReverse`, `ReversalBlockedException` + `ReversalShortage`, `ProblemCodes`. Tests: domain +22, API +10 (`UnpostedAmountTests`, reversal cases in `InsufficientStockTests`, `IssueEstimateTests`, `ReversalDeficitTests`).
- Needs from fe-framework: (1) a 409 with `code = "reversal_blocked"` is NOT a stale document and NOT the E5 post conflict: show its `detail` (E6) inside the reverse dialog and keep the typed reason; `isStaleDocumentError` must test `code` (absent on a stale 409), not only the missing `productId`. (2) Show the value of unposted issues from `totalAmount` (label it estimated when `amountIsEstimate`) in the approvals inbox, the lists and the document page; show `approvalTriggers` as the "why approval" text. (3) Regenerate the client.
- Decisions others must respect: (1) Issue lines carry the average cost seen at the last save or submit (`unitCost` of an unposted issue line is no longer 0); posting still books the real cost over it. The value tested against approval rules is the one stored at submit (the rule check at approve time reuses it). (2) The reversal check covers the whole document before anything moves.
- Open conflicts: none. A document submitted before this change keeps zero line costs until it is saved or submitted again (no production data exists).

#### Contract changes for the frontend (part 2)
- `DocumentDto` (every response with a document): new `amountIsEstimate` (bool: unposted issue, `totalAmount` and line `unitCost` are estimates at the average cost of the last save or submit) and `approvalTriggers` (`[{ metric: "Amount"|"Quantity", threshold, actual, roleRequired }]`, filled for a Submitted or Approved document that an approval rule held back; empty otherwise, and when only a negative-stock override sent it to approval). `totalAmount` of an unposted issue is no longer 0.
- `DocumentListItem`: new `amountIsEstimate`; `totalAmount` of an unposted issue is no longer 0 (inbox Value column).
- `POST /api/documents/{id}/reverse`: when the reversal would make stock negative (negative stock off) the answer is `409`, title "Reversal blocked", `code: "reversal_blocked"`, `detail` = UX E6 copy naming every product (one product: "Can't reverse RCV-... It would make DEMO-001 negative (-3 PCS). Reverse or adjust the later issues first, or ask an Admin."; several: "... It would make 2 products negative: A (-3 PCS), B (-6 PCS). ..."), and `shortages: [{ productId, sku, uomCode, balance }]` (balance = resulting negative quantity in the base unit). It has no `productId` member. The post-time stock conflict (`409` "Insufficient stock") now also carries `code: "insufficient_stock"` next to `productId`, `available`, `requested`.

Code: `ILowStockReader` + `EfLowStockReader` + `LowStockQuery` (Infrastructure), `AuditEntities.Known/Matching`, `AuditActions.Known`, `GET /api/audit/filters`, `ImportProductsHandler` (no side-created categories or units), migration `AuditSearchIndexes` (two indexes on `AuditLogs`; `Down` drops them). Tests: low stock, import reference data, audit search (API +37).

(2) Audit log screen: replace the exact "Object" box by the new `search` box (entity name fragment or document number) or a dropdown from `GET /api/audit/filters`; add an Action filter to hide sign-ins; the User filter is "starts with". (3) Import preview: category and unit errors are row errors now (the wizard text "the category must exist" is true). (4) Regenerate the client.

(2) `GET /api/stock?lowOnly=true` still lists stock rows only (a never-stocked product has none), so its total can be smaller than the low-stock list; the list, the Home count and the export always agree. #### Contract changes for the frontend (part 3)
- `GET /api/stock/low-stock`, `GET /api/reports/low-stock/export`, `GET /api/stock/summary` (`lowCount`): include active products with a safety level above zero and no balance row anywhere (on hand 0, shortfall = safety stock) and negative balances. `LowStockAlert` has new `isNegative`. `GET /api/stock/summary`: `skuCount` ("products with stock") counts products with a positive quantity somewhere in the caller's warehouses only (zero and negative are not counted); `totalValue` stays net of negative lines.
- `GET /api/stock` rows: new `isNegative`; `isLow` is also true for a negative balance. `lowOnly=true` includes negative balances.
- `POST /api/import/products` (preview and commit): an unknown category is the row error "Category 'X' doesn't exist. Add it first or fix the name."; an unknown unit "Unit 'X' doesn't exist. Use the code or name of an existing unit, or add it first." (units match by code or name, any case); a non-numeric safety stock "Safety stock 'abc' is not a number."; a repeated SKU "SKU 'x' also appears in row N." Nothing is created on the side. `POST /api/import/opening-stock`: "Quantity 'ten' is not a number." / "Unit cost 'cheap' is not a number." The product template's sample barcodes are now `SAMPLE-0000000001,SAMPLE-0000000002`.
- `GET /api/audit`: `entityType` is a case-insensitive fragment of the entity name ("doc" finds StockDocument); new `entityId` (starts with: document number or id), new `action` (starts with: "document.", "login"), new `search` (an entity name fragment OR an id prefix in one box); `actor` is "starts with". New `GET /api/audit/filters` (`audit.read`): `{ entityTypes: string[], actions: string[] }` for dropdowns.
- Audit details (`before` / `after` JSON): enums are names (`"Status":"Submitted"`), not numbers. Old rows keep their numbers.

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, integration-test-engineer, orchestrator (QA backend fixes, part 4 of 4)
- Produced (branch `feature/qa-backend-fixes-4`, based on part 3): input limits and messages (EA-16, EA-17, EA-18, EA-30, PS-15), `If-Match` required (EA-19, PS-25), stale-document details (PS-08, EA-28), account lock message (EA-13), readable 429 (EA-24), `users.read` only for Admin (EA-12), stock-conflict sentence with names (NV-06), the per-finding status list `docs/qa/fix-status-backend.md`. Code: `FilterRuleExtensions` + 12 small query validators, `StaleDocumentException` + `StaleDocumentBehavior`, `ShortageNamingBehavior`, `PreconditionRequiredException`, `AccountLockedException`, `ProblemResponses.WriteRateLimitedAsync`, `UserWarehouses`, required purpose on issues in the domain. No schema change. Tests: API +38 (`IfMatchRequiredTests`, `InputValidationTests`, lockout, 429, stock-conflict wording), domain +1. The smoke test (`tests/smoke/smoke.mjs`) now sends `If-Match: *` on document commands and a purpose on issues; it passed (84 checks) against a local API.
- Needs from fe-framework (BREAKING, do before this reaches users): (1) send `If-Match` with the document's `concurrencyToken` on EVERY document command (save/PUT, submit, approve, reject, cancel, post, reverse), including the call that follows a create in the same click (use the token of the response you just got); a missing header is now `428`. (2) Stale 409: use `code = "document_changed"`, `changedBy` and `changedAt` (UTC) for the E4 sentence; clear the banner after Reload. (3) Login: show `detail` for a 401 that has `code = "account_locked"` (E7); 429: show `detail`, wait `retryAfterSeconds`. (4) An issue must be saved with a `purpose` (the form already requires it). (5) Search boxes: `maxlength` 100; date filters: `from` after `to` is a 400 with a message under `errors.to`. (6) Regenerate the client.
- Decisions others must respect: (1) `*` in `If-Match` is the explicit opt-out; do not use it in the web app. (2) `users.read` is Admin only now; no Owner/Supervisor screen may list users. (3) Duplicate product lines are still accepted (availability adds them up); say if they should be refused. (4) The login limiter is still per client address.
- Open conflicts: none.

#### Contract changes for the frontend (part 4)
- All `PUT /api/documents/{id}` and `POST /api/documents/{id}/(submit|approve|reject|cancel|post|reverse)`: `If-Match` required. Missing: `428`, title "Precondition required", `code: "if_match_required"`. Accepted forms: `"token"`, `token`, `W/"token"`, `*`. A stale token: `409` with `code: "document_changed"`, `changedBy` (display name, may be absent) and `changedAt` (UTC) next to the unchanged detail sentence.
- Stock conflict (`409` "Insufficient stock", submit or post): `detail` is now "Not enough stock for {sku} in {warehouse name}: {available} {unit} available, {requested} needed." (was ids); `productId`, `available`, `requested`, `code` unchanged.
- `POST /api/auth/login`: a correct password on a locked account is `401` with `detail` "Too many failed attempts. Try again in N minutes.", `code: "account_locked"`, `retryAfterMinutes`; a wrong password or unknown user is unchanged (generic sentence, no code).
- Any `429`: ProblemDetails with `Retry-After` header, `code: "rate_limited"`, `retryAfterSeconds`, `detail` "Too many requests. Try again in N seconds."
- Lists and exports (`/api/products`, `/api/stock`, `/api/partners`, `/api/users`, `/api/documents`, `/api/audit`, `/api/reports/stock-summary/export`): `search` longer than 100 characters is `400` with `errors.search`; `/api/products/lookup?code=` longer than 64 is `400`. `from` later than `to` on documents, ledger, audit, movements and the movements/ledger exports is `400` with `errors.to`.
- `POST|PUT /api/users`: unknown warehouse id is `400` "Warehouse does not exist."; `username` may contain only letters, digits and `. _ - @ +` (else `errors.username`).
- `POST /api/documents/issues`, `PUT` of an issue: `purpose` is required (`400`, `errors.purpose` on create); it used to default to Sale.
- Name fields (product name/SKU, partner, warehouse code/name, category, unit code/name, username): too long answers "{Field} is too long (maximum N characters)."; empty keeps "... is required."
- `GET /api/users` is `403` for Owner and Supervisor (permission `users.read` is Admin only; it is gone from their `permissions`).

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, integration-test-engineer, ui-ux-designer, qa-edge-accessibility, orchestrator (cost and value visibility, EA-23)
- Produced (branch `feature/cost-visibility`, product-owner decision 1; independent of the signed-stock-effect PR): Warehouse staff see no cost or value anywhere, the Clerk sees cost on documents but not stock value, Supervisor, Owner and Admin see everything. Two permissions, `cost.view` and `valuation.view`, in `Permissions`, written once as `[VisibleWith(permission)]` (`Ims.Domain.VisibleWithAttribute`) on the property, entity property, export column or sort key that holds the cost, and applied by one pipeline step: `CostVisibilityBehavior` (`Ims.Application/Common/Visibility`: `ResponseShaper`, `RequestGuard`, `AuditPayloadScrubber`, `ShapingState`, `CallerPermissions`), `IReportWriter` + `ReportColumn` for Excel, `AuditFields`, `ApprovalTriggerDto`, `IReceiptCostDefaults` + `StockDocument.ApplyKnownReceiptCosts`. Docs: `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 1.1 and new 1.4, `docs/architecture/backend.md` (Cost and value visibility), `docs/qa/fix-status-backend.md` (EA-23). Tests: domain +23 (`Visibility/*`, `KnownReceiptCostTests`, permissions), architecture +10 (`CostVisibilityCoverageTests`), API +35 (`Tests/Visibility/CostVisibilityMatrixTests`, `StaffReceiptCostTests`, `Support/MoneyScan`, one pipeline-order test), smoke test has a cost-visibility block (and its receipts are entered by the clerk now).
- Needs from fe-framework: adapt `web/` to the section below (nullable cost and value fields, hide what is `null`, the `ApprovalTriggerDto` schema, the staff receipt form without a cost field, the Home value tile), regenerate the client.
- Needs from ui-ux-designer: check the wording for a Warehouse staff user when approval is needed because of an amount ("Needs approval", the role that approves, no number); and decide whether stock received only by Warehouse staff (cost zero until a Clerk or Supervisor receipt brings a cost) needs a cost-correction feature.
- Needs from integration-test-engineer: review `Tests/Visibility`; `Support/RoleMatrix.cs` now lists the two permissions.
- Decisions others must respect: (1) Cost and value are tagged where they are declared; never filter by role name. A new response property whose name looks like cost, value, amount, price, worth or threshold must carry `[VisibleWith]` and be nullable, or `CostVisibilityCoverageTests` fails (a name that is not money goes in its `NotMoney` list with a reason). (2) Exports and templates are written through `IReportWriter` only. (3) A payload of the audit log that names money uses a constant of `AuditFields`. (4) Handlers see full values; only the outermost request is shaped, so a handler may read the full result of a command it sends. (5) Staff receipts: see below.
- Open conflicts: none. Not done: a way to correct the cost of stock that was received by Warehouse staff (it carries the last known cost, or zero if the product was never stocked there, until a receipt with a cost comes in); the Owner's approve and reject commands have no test of their own (same code path as the Supervisor's, who is covered).

#### Receipts by Warehouse staff (the decision asked for)
`docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 3.1 has the Warehouse staff receive stock by quantity only (no cost field), and Q6 made cost optional, so Warehouse staff can still create, submit and post receipts. A receipt line saved by anyone without `cost.view` takes the last known cost: the product's average cost in that warehouse, or zero if the product was never stocked there, whatever `unitCost` the request carries (ignored, not an error). Receiving at the average cost leaves the average unchanged, so the receipt cannot pull the valuation towards zero (the existing rule, a missing cost is zero, would). Clerk, Supervisor and Admin enter the cost as before. Applies on create and on every save of the draft (not on submit, so a cost a supervisor entered on a staff draft survives).

#### Contract changes for the frontend (cost and value visibility)
- `permissions` (login, refresh, `GET /api/auth/me`) can contain `cost.view` and `valuation.view`: Admin, Owner, Supervisor both; Clerk `cost.view` only; Warehouse staff neither. Show a cost only with `cost.view`, a value only with `valuation.view`; a field that is `null` is "not for you" (render nothing, not 0.00).
- These fields are always present in the JSON but `null` without the permission (the OpenAPI type is `number | null`, still listed as required): **`cost.view`**: `DocumentDto.totalAmount`, `DocumentLineDto.unitCost`, `DocumentListItem.totalAmount`, `MovementRow.unitCost` and `amount`, `ApprovalRuleDto.amountThreshold`, `approvalTriggers[].threshold` and `actual` of an amount rule. **`valuation.view`**: `StockRow.avgCost` and `value`, `LedgerRow.unitCost`, `StockSummary.totalValue` (the Home value tile). `amountIsEstimate` stays (a flag, not a value).
- The schema `ApprovalTrigger` is now `ApprovalTriggerDto` (same members, `threshold` and `actual` nullable). For a quantity rule the numbers are shown to everyone; for an amount rule the Warehouse staff get `metric: "Amount"`, `roleRequired` and null numbers. `requiresApproval`, `isLarge`, `canAct`, `allowedActions` do not depend on the role's cost rights.
- `GET /api/stock` and `GET /api/reports/stock-summary/export` with `sortBy=Value` answer `400` (`errors.sortBy`, "Sorting by value is not available to your role.") without `valuation.view`; hide that sort option.
- Excel: a column without the caller's permission is left out of the file (header and cells). Today only roles that hold both permissions can call the exports, so nothing visible changes. The opening-stock import template has its `UnitCost` column only for a caller with `cost.view`.
- `POST /api/documents/receipts` and `PUT /api/documents/{id}` of a receipt by a caller without `cost.view`: `lines[].unitCost` in the request is ignored; the line takes the last known cost (see above). Do not show a cost field to the Warehouse staff.
- The audit log (`/api/audit`, `/api/audit/filters`) is Owner and Admin only as before (`403` for Staff and Clerk); the cost keys are also removed from `before` / `after` for a caller who lacks the permission.
- Unchanged: counts (`/api/documents/counts`), low-stock rows and their export, the products, partners, warehouses and users endpoints carry no cost.

### 2026-10-10 behavior-test-engineer -> orchestrator, be-framework, be-architect, fe-framework, project-manager
- Produced (two PRs, the second stacked on the first because the PR guard caps a PR at 1,500 lines): `e2e/` (own `package.json`, Playwright 1.x, TypeScript, `@axe-core/playwright`; nothing added to `web/`). PR 1 `feature/e2e-playwright`: harness (`src/config.ts`, `src/api.ts` seeding through the public API, `src/fixtures.ts` sign-in through the real login form, `src/pages/*` page objects, `src/rate-limit.ts`, `src/global-setup.ts`, `scripts/run-local.mjs`) and scenarios 01 to 05 (receive, shortfall, approval with override and self-approval block, reversal blocked/allowed, stale document). PR 2 `feature/e2e-playwright-scenarios`: scenarios 06 to 12 (role matrix and Clerk partners, barcode/SKU scan, session restore and storage, language switch keeps the form, phone 360x740 and no horizontal scroll, axe, low stock) and `.github/workflows/e2e.yml` (manual + weekly, never on pull requests). How to run: `e2e/README.md`.
- Result: 33 scenarios, all passing; four full runs in a row against a local API (throw-away LocalDB database) and a production web build. Defects found: none open (no `test.fixme`). Every QA fix named in `docs/qa/fix-status-*.md` that the brief asked for has a regression test: duplicate draft on save (NV-01), double submit via Back in the Post dialog (NV-02), Add item focus (NV-03, PS-05), reversal blocked message (PS-01), stale banner (PS-08), role rules (menus, forbidden page, Clerk partners, self-approval block), reason length (PS-09).
- Needs from be-framework / be-architect (observation, not a bug in a requirement): `POST /api/auth/login` AND `POST /api/auth/refresh` share one limiter, 20 per minute per client address (`Limits.LoginRateLimitPerMinute`, policy `login`). Every full page load of a signed-in tab calls refresh, so one office behind one NAT address, or a user pressing reload, can lock everybody out of start-up for a minute (the page then shows the login screen with "Too many requests"). Suggest: a separate, larger budget for refresh (or key it on the refresh cookie), keep 20 for password attempts. The suite works around it by navigating inside the app and pacing sign-ins (`e2e/src/rate-limit.ts`).
- Needs from fe-framework: none. Stable hooks used: roles and accessible names only; the few CSS hooks are in `e2e/src/pages/shell.ts` (`nav.shell-nav`, `nav.shell-tabbar`, because the navigation's accessible name is translated; `app-toast`; `app-page-header app-status-chip`).
- Needs from project-manager: add "E2E suite (Playwright)" to `TASKS.md` and decide whether `.github/workflows/e2e.yml` should also become a required weekly check.
- Decisions others must respect: (1) E2E lives in the top-level `e2e/` folder, not `web/apps/main-e2e` (the earlier note in this log and in `docs/architecture/frontend.md` section 6 are superseded; fe-architect: please update that sentence). (2) The suite starts nothing itself; it reads `IMS_WEB_URL`, `IMS_API_URL` and `IMS_E2E_PASSWORD_<ROLE>` and never prints a password. (3) It runs with one worker: two scenarios switch the global "allow negative stock" setting (restored in `afterEach`) and all sign-ins share the limiter above. (4) A scenario that finds a product bug is marked `test.fixme('... (finding id)')` and reported here, never skipped silently. (5) Axe exceptions go in `KNOWN` in `e2e/tests/11-accessibility.spec.ts` with a finding id; the list is empty today.
- Open conflicts: none.

### 2026-10-10 be-framework -> fe-framework, integration-test-engineer, be-dba, orchestrator (signed stock effect in the movements report)
- Produced (branch `feature/signed-stock-effect`, product-owner decision 2; independent of the cost-visibility PR): the movements report (page, total and Excel export) has a signed `stockEffect` next to the unsigned quantity, and a reversed document now shows a reversal row. Code: `StockDirection` (Domain, the sign convention), `StockDocument.ReversedAt` + `MarkReversed(reason, utcNow)`, migration `AddDocumentReversedAt` (new column, backfilled from the reversal ledger entries, plus the filtered index `IX_StockDocument_ReversedAt`; `Down` drops both; tested up, down, up), `IMovementReader` + `EfMovementReader` (the page, total and export read the posting rows and the reversal rows through it), `MovementEntry`, `PostingMovementsSpec`, `ReversalMovementsSpec`, `ReversalMovementsCountSpec`; `MovementsPageSpec` and `MovementsSpec` are gone. Tests: domain +9 (`StockDirectionTests`, `ReversedAt`), API +5 (`Tests/Reports/SignedStockEffectTests`: signs, reversal rows, the balance identity after receive, issue, a transfer (issue plus receipt, there is no transfer document yet) and reversals, in the page, the export and the ledger; the date range; page walks).
- Needs from fe-framework: show `stockEffect` (signed, base units) beside the quantity on the Receipt-issue detail tab, and a "Reversal" label from `isReversal`; regenerate the client.
- Needs from be-dba: look at `IX_StockDocument_ReversedAt` and the merged query (`docs/architecture/data-and-performance-rules.md` sections 8 and 9). Measured on the 130k-ledger-row seed with 500 reversed documents: first page 438 reads before, 225 after; offset 5,000 about the same as before. Not measured at 1M rows; the posting branch is the same query as before.
- Decisions others must respect: (1) A reversed document has TWO rows per line: the posting (status Reversed, effect as at posting) and a reversal row (`isReversal = true`, opposite sign), so the effects of a product over a date range add up to the change in its stock balance. This is the only way the sum can be exact, because a reversal can fall in a later period than the posting; it is a change to the number of rows the report returns (the QA note "movements 1,112 + 3 reversal rows = ledger 1,115" is now true by construction). (2) `postedAt` of a reversal row is the time of the reversal and the `from`/`to` filter dates a reversal row by that time. (3) The order of the existing (posting) rows relative to each other and every existing index are unchanged; the merged order is `At DESC, DocumentId DESC, LineId, IsReversal DESC`. (4) `StockDirection` and the SQL expression in `MovementProjection` must stay equal (a transfer will need a direction per line); `SignedStockEffectTests` fails if they drift.
- Open conflicts: none.

#### Contract changes for the frontend (signed stock effect)
- `GET /api/reports/movements` (`MovementRow`): new `stockEffect` (number, base units, signed: positive adds stock, negative removes it; receipt +, issue -, the reversal row of a receipt -, of an issue +) and `isReversal` (bool). `qty`, `baseQty`, `unitCost`, `amount` are unchanged and unsigned. `total` counts the reversal rows too.
- A document with status `Reversed` now appears twice per line: the posting row (`isReversal: false`) and the reversal row (`isReversal: true`, `postedAt` = when it was reversed, same `docNo`, `status: "Reversed"`). Rows are newest first by `postedAt`, then document id; the reversal row comes before the posting it undoes when the times are equal.
- `GET /api/reports/movements/export`: new column "Stock effect" after "Base qty" (same numbers, same row order as the page). Headers: Posted (MYT), Document, Type, Status, Warehouse, Partner, SKU, Product, Qty, Unit, Base qty, Stock effect, Unit cost, Amount.
- `GET /api/documents` and `/api/documents/{id}` are unchanged. No other endpoint changes.

### 2026-10-11 be-framework -> fe-framework, orchestrator, ui-ux-designer, integration-test-engineer, be-dba (supervisor re-test: average cost, money precision, backend findings)
- Note: PR 3 is being redone on a new branch by another agent; the PR 3 items in this entry (low stock, search, ledger balance, audit, reversed-by, Excel Reversal column) describe its first version and must be re-checked against the new one.
- Produced (three pull requests, each based on `main`, merge in this order): **1** `feature/avg-cost-and-money-precision` (PR-01, internal cost precision, migration `AverageCostMarkersAndCostScale`; this entry), **2** `feature/avg-cost-and-money-precision-2` (money has exactly 2 decimals, stored line amount; its own entry further up the log, inserted before the first 2026-10-10 entry so that the PRs merge without a conflict), **3** `feature/avg-cost-and-money-precision-3` (PR-04, PR-05, PR-08, PR-10, PR-11, PS-12, PS-14, migration `DocumentSearchIndexAndReversedBy`, `docs/qa/fix-status-backend-2.md`). Per finding: `docs/qa/fix-status-backend-2.md`.
- Rules chosen (others must respect them): (1) **Reversing a receipt**: the balance remembers the line that brought stock in last (`StockBalance.LastReceiptLineId`, set by EVERY stock-in, also one at the average and the return of a reversed issue); while the reversed receipt is still that line the average goes back to what it was before it (recorded on the line), otherwise the receipt's `qty x cost` leaves the stock value and the rest is shared, otherwise the average is kept. Equal averages prove nothing. `docs/design/mvp-ux.md` 1.5, `docs/architecture/backend.md` (Costing and reversal). (2) **Internal numbers are not money.** The cost per base unit and the average keep 10 decimals (`Limits.CostDecimals`, `decimal(22,10)`, rounded half away from zero); every amount a person sees follows the money rule of PR 2. (3) **Unit cost range**: at most 1,000,000,000 entered (400 `Unit cost cannot be more than 1,000,000,000.`), and at most 999,999,999,999 per base unit after conversion (400 `Unit cost is too large for this unit of measure.`); never a 500. (4) **Low stock** is one rule, `Ims.Domain.LowStockRule`.
- Needs from fe-framework: adapt `web/` to the section below and regenerate the client.
- Needs from ui-ux-designer / product owner: the decisions at the end of `docs/qa/fix-status-backend-2.md`.
- Needs from be-dba: (a) **Maintenance window for PR 1.** Migration `AverageCostMarkersAndCostScale` changes the type of three columns (`StockLedgerEntry.UnitCost`, `StockDocumentLine.UnitCost`, `StockBalance.AvgCost`) from `decimal(18,6)` to `decimal(22,10)` (storage 9 to 13 bytes per value) under a schema-modification lock, and adds four nullable columns. Stop the API first. It rewrites the three tables: about 3 minutes on 1.5 million ledger rows with a cold cache (ledger 128 s, document lines 64 s; Down about 21 s), so plan a maintenance window of several minutes, scaled to the size of the ledger. Rebuild the indexes of the three tables afterwards (`docs/dba/07-maintenance.sql`). No value can overflow: the integer part is 12 digits before and after. (b) Look at `IX_StockDocument_Search` and `IX_StockDocument_ReversedBy` (migration `DocumentSearchIndexAndReversedBy`), measured on a 21,300-document seed in `docs/architecture/data-and-performance-rules.md` section 9. (c) `docs/dba/15-repair-reversal-average.sql` repairs, once, the averages that the old reversal left contaminated (report first, then `-v Apply=1`).
- Open conflicts: none. A conflict-free order exists: 1, 2, 3 (the three branches merge into one tree without a conflict in any order).

#### Contract changes for the frontend (re-test round)
- **Cost and average precision (PR 1).** `unitCost`, `avgCost` and ledger `unitCost` come from columns with 10 decimals, and a computed amount (`quantity x cost`, a total, a value) can carry up to 14 decimals, until PR 2 is deployed. Do not format with a fixed number of decimals before PR 2; after PR 2 amounts (`amount`, totals, `value`) are rounded to 2 decimals and the per-base unit prices (`avgCost`, ledger and movements `unitCost`, the new `baseUnitCost`) to 4, half away from zero, and the Excel exports hold numbers formatted `#,##0.00` and `#,##0.0000` (see the entry of PR 2). PR 1 alone changes no field name.
- **How PR 1 and PR 2 fit.** PR 1 stores more places inside (an internal precision); PR 2 decides what leaves the API (money = 2 decimals, `ResponseShaper`). Deployed together, a user sees amounts with exactly 2 decimals and unit prices with at most 4. Deployed alone, PR 1 shows the longer numbers it always computed, only longer. The values that stay internal: the average and the cost per base unit in the database; the numbers a screen shows are money (2 decimals) or a unit price (4).
- **Unit cost range.** `POST`/`PUT` document requests answer `400` under `lines[n].unitCost` for a cost above 1,000,000,000 ("Unit cost cannot be more than 1,000,000,000.") and `400` ("Unit cost is too large for this unit of measure.") when the cost spread over the base unit would not fit. The opening-stock import reports the first as a row error. Limit the cost input to 1,000,000,000.
- **Reversal.** No contract change; a reversal answers as before (`reversal_blocked` by quantity). After the reversal the stock row's `avgCost` and `value` are what they were before the receipt.
- **`GET /api/stock` and `GET /api/reports/stock-summary/export`.** `lowOnly=true` is now the low-stock definition: it also lists active products with a safety level that were never stocked (one row per active warehouse in scope, `qtyOnHand` 0, `avgCost` 0, `value` 0, `isLow` true), so its `total` equals `GET /api/stock/low-stock`. `isLow` is false for an inactive product. New `sortBy=Name` (the default is still `Sku`; the design wants the screen to open sorted by product name). Every sort is total.
- **`GET /api/stock/low-stock`.** Worst first by the share missing from the safety level (then units, SKU, warehouse code); was by units.
- **`GET /api/stock/ledger?productId=...`.** New `balanceAfter` on every row (number, base units): the balance of that product in that warehouse right after the movement. `null` when no `productId` is given.
- **`GET /api/documents?search=`.** Matches the document number, the remark and the partner name (was the number only).
- **`DocumentDto`.** New `reversedBy`, `reversedByName`, `reversedAt` (null until reversed) for "Reversed by {name} on {date}".
- **`GET /api/audit`.** New `hideSignIns=true` (leaves out `login.success`, `login.failed`, `login.locked`, `login.lockout`, `logout`). A settings save writes one `settings.updated` entry (entity `SystemSetting`, id `all`) with the old and new values of all settings; the per-key `updated` rows no longer exist.
- **Excel.** The movements export has a new column "Reversal" after "Status" ("Yes" on the reversal row of a reversed document): Posted (MYT), Document, Type, Status, Reversal, Warehouse, Partner, SKU, Product, Qty, Unit, Base qty, Stock effect, Unit cost, Amount.
- Unchanged: the permissions, `allowedActions`, the reversal-blocked answer, the paging contract, the If-Match rules.

### 2026-10-11 be-architect -> orchestrator, be-dba, be-framework, fe-architect, integration-test-engineer, behavior-test-engineer, security-engineer (ADR: GUID keys)
- Produced: `docs/architecture/adr-guid-keys.md` (status Proposed). Recommendation: option B, a hybrid. Keep the int (bigint on ledger and audit) clustered keys; add `PublicId` (GUID, unique, unfiltered index) on 9 tables a client addresses (User, Warehouse, Partner, Category, Uom, Product, ApprovalRule, StockDocument, Attachment); API, URLs, client and logs use only `PublicId`; cursors become opaque strings; client-minted ids for documents and attachments make creates idempotent. Ledger, audit, lines, balances, tokens, sequences, settings and join tables keep their keys (GUID there is a bad idea: append order is the data model, +80 bytes per ledger row). Cost about 85 agent-hours in 12 PRs (option A about 115). Additive migration, no baseline reset needed.
- Facts others should know: the keys are `int` (14 tables), not bigint; only the ledger and audit are bigint. SQL Server sorts `uniqueidentifier` by the last group first, so UUIDv7 (`Guid.CreateVersion7`, browser UUIDs) is random in a SQL Server index (checked with a query in tempdb); only EF's sequential generator or `NEWSEQUENTIALID()` is index-friendly. `TriggerNames.LedgerAppendOnly` (`TR_StockLedgerEntries_AppendOnly`) is unused and does not match the live trigger (`TR_StockLedgerEntry_AppendOnly`).
- Needs from the product owner (via orchestrator): D1 (is offline creation or data exchange between deployments committed within about a year) and D2 (must scoped users be unable to infer other warehouses' activity from ids), section 8 of the ADR.
- Needs from be-dba: reconcile `docs/dba/guid-pk/` results with section 7 (predictions to check); `results/` was empty when I finished. Note that the A2 and B2 variants use a v7-shaped value, which I predict fragments like NEWID().
- Needs from fe-architect / fe-framework: until the decision, write new code (R06 preferences, R17 to R20 Home, recents, palette) with ids as opaque strings; no `Number(id)`. Section 3.2 lists the breaking contract changes and section 6.3 the placement against the refresh waves.
- Needs from be-framework (independent of the key decision): SEC-11, return 404 for out-of-scope by-id reads.
- Decisions others must respect: none yet (Proposed). `docs/architecture/backend.md` is not changed until the decision.
- Open conflicts: none.

Measured inputs: variant C and the generator check from `docs/dba/guid-pk/results/`; A and B figures are my estimates (C scaled by key width) until the DBA variants finish.
- Findings that matter: EF sequential GUIDs are index-friendly but countable (consecutive ids differ by one), so a GUID PK cannot be index-friendly and leak-free at once; only the hybrid is. Most "user-defined unique codes" already exist (Username, Warehouse.Code, Uom.Code, Sku, document No, Category/Partner Name); only `Partner.Code` would be new. The earlier Express buffer-pool remark is withdrawn (this instance targets about 40 GB).
- Recommendation: option 1 (hybrid, with D2 hardening, about 85 agent-hours). Option 2b (GUID clustered PK on master and document tables, int alternate keys for FKs from big tables, about 95 hours, +4 % size) only if the SQL PK itself must be a GUID. Never a GUID key on ledger or audit.
